AI 資金壓力拖累科技股，惟產業需求仍具韌性
2026 年 10 月 23 日
一、 市場動態
1. 費半再度重挫，科技股成為美股主要賣壓來源：美國股市主要指數 10 月 22 日跌多漲
少， 道瓊工業指數上漲0.02%，收52,867.98 點；S&P 500 指數下跌 1.21%，收7,663.43
點；Nasdaq 綜合指數下跌 2.60%，收 26,008.86 點，創 6 月 5 日以來最大單日跌幅
。費城半導體指數重挫 6.72%，收 10,731.98 點，跌幅明顯大於主要股價指數，顯示
AI 及半導體族群仍是本波市場修正的主要壓力來源。

2. AI 相關企業全面承壓 ， 資金流向非科技股及公債 ：10 月 22 日 Meta 下跌約 7%、Lam
Research 跌幅逾一成，Nvidia 下跌 4.4%，其他 AI 相關股票亦明顯走低，顯示市場
對 AI 資本支出及投資報酬的疑慮已由大型科技平台延伸至半導體與基礎建設供應商
。不過，道瓊上漲 0.02%，羅素 2000 指數亦小幅收紅，反映資金並未全面撤出股市
；美國 10 年期公債殖利率則因避險需求跌破 5%，顯示資金由高評價科技股轉向其他
產業及避險資產。
3. 中東地緣政治風險升溫，油價攀至 5 月以來高點：中東能源運輸及供給風險 10 月 22
日再度升高，布蘭特原油價格上漲約 3.76%，升至每桶 113 美元附近，創 5 月以來高
點。油價攀升除使市場重新關注能源成本對通膨與 Fed 政策路徑的影響，也可能增加
AI 資料中心的電力及營運成本。中東情勢雖非本次半導體股重挫的唯一因素，但在市
場已擔憂 AI 投資效益的情況下，能源價格上揚進一步壓抑投資人風險偏好。

二、 下跌原因分析：
1. 殖利率回落難抵科技股下跌，顯示修正因素出現轉變：10 月 14 日費半重挫主要受到
美國 9 月 CPI 高於預期、升息疑慮及美債殖利率盤中急升影響，惟當日 10 年期殖利
率收盤反而小幅下降；10 月 22 日情況則更加明顯，美國 10 年期公債殖利率因避險
需求盤中跌破 5%，費半仍大跌 6.72%。這顯示市場關注焦點已由單純的利率與估值
壓力，逐步轉向 AI 資本支出的資金來源、融資能力及投資回收期。因此，即使後續殖
利率回落，若企業仍無法證明AI 投資能轉化為足夠營收與現金流，科技股評價仍可能
持續受到壓抑。
2. Meta 自由現金流轉負 ，AI 投資成本由預期轉為實際財務壓力 ：Meta 第三季營收 646.0
億美元、年增 26%，顯示核心業務仍維持強勁成長；但資本支出達 349.1 億美元，超
過同期營業現金流 327.9 億美元，使自由現金流由去年同期正 106.3 億美元轉為負
21.2 億美元。公司亦將全年資本支出預估調整至 1,350～1,450 億美元，並宣布設立

無表決權 Class C 股票，增加未來資本運用彈性。這反映 AI 投資規模已開始超越部分

### Page 2

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
大型科技企業當期現金創造能力，但 Meta 仍持有 824.5 億美元現金、約當現金及有
價證券，不能將單季自由現金流轉負直接解讀為財務危機。
3. 資本稀缺逐漸成為 AI 擴張瓶頸，新興雲端業者面臨更大壓力：AI 發展需要持續投入
GPU、伺服器、資料中心及電力基礎建設，龐大資金需求使融資能力逐漸成為決定企
業成長速度的重要因素。相較具備穩定營業現金流與龐大資產規模的大型科技平台，
新興 AI 雲端服務商如 CoreWeave 及 Nebius 高度依賴外部融資，因而更容易受到借
貸成本、信用利差及投資人風險偏好變化影響。我們認為，這次修正並不代表 AI 運算

需求突然消失，而是市場開始重新評估不同企業能否負擔下一階段的擴張成本。
4. 半導體設備財報強勁卻遭賣壓，市場提高獲利兌現標準：Lam Research 於 10 月 21
日公布截至 2026 年 9 月季度（FY2027 Q1）營收 82.19 億美元，年增 54.4%、季增
22%，非 GAAP 營業利益率達 40.2%，下一季營收指引為 86 億美元、上下浮動 4 億
美元。然而，即使營運仍快速成長，10 月 22 日股價仍大幅下跌。我們研判，市場已
由過去重視 AI 訂單與營收成長，轉向同時檢視未來成長能否超越原先偏高預期，以及
AI 投資是否具備足夠的財務報酬。當股價已反映高度成長期待，即使財報表現良好，
也可能因評價調整與籌碼降溫而承壓。
5. 油價再度上漲，Fed 政策風險仍可能限制科技股評價：儘管 10 月 22 日美債殖利率因
避險需求下滑，但能源價格上漲仍可能延緩通膨降溫，使 Fed 後續政策路徑維持不確
定性。若油價持續高檔，不僅可能推升企業運輸、電力及營運成本，也將增加通膨預

期，進而限制長天期美債殖利率的下行空間。因此，科技股短線同時面臨AI 投資報酬
受到重新檢視 ， 以及能源價格與利率政策不確定的雙重壓力 。 後續10 月 28 日 FOMC
會後聲明及主席記者會，將是評估利率環境的重要依據。
三、 市場看法：
1. 股市下跌但多數個股上漲 ， 顯示賣壓集中於大型科技股 ：10 月 22 日 S&P 500 指數下
跌 1.21%，但根據美聯社（AP）報導，約三分之二成分股逆勢上漲，追蹤 S&P 500
等權重指數的 ETF（RSP）更上漲 0.73%，道瓊工業指數亦小幅收紅，呈現市值加權
指數與市場廣度明顯背離的現象 。這反映本波修正主要集中於權重較高的 AI 及大型科
技股，而非整體美股同步走弱。相較上週以防禦性類股輪動為主的情況，本週更值得
注意的是市場對高資本支出及高評價科技企業的重新定價，資金仍持續流向其他產業
，而非全面性風險撤離。只要賣壓未進一步擴散，現階段仍較接近科技股評價調整與
類股輪動，而非整體美股多頭格局反轉。
2. 台積電財測再度上修，顯示 AI 需求尚未出現結構性反轉：台積電 10 月 15 日法說會

將 2026 年美元營收成長預估由原先略高於 40%上修至約 44%， 第三季營收達新台幣
1.49 兆元、年增 50.9%，稅後純益年增 68.7%，每股盈餘 29.43 元、同樣年增 68.7%
；第四季美元營收預估為 523～543 億美元，以中間值計算季增約 14%。此外，Lam
Research 截至 9 月季度營收年增 54.4%，亦顯示半導體設備需求仍強勁。前述這些

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
數據與近期科技股重挫形成明顯對比 ， 反映目前市場主要擔憂AI 投資回收及股票評價
，而非半導體終端需求同步惡化。只要大型雲端服務商未全面縮減AI 資本支出，先進
製程與設備需求中期仍具支撐。
3. AI 投資進入報酬驗證階段，營收成長能否追上資本投入成為關鍵：我們認為，AI 產業
目前正由過去的資本支出擴張階段，逐步轉向投資報酬率與自由現金流的驗證階段。
以 Meta 為例，第三季營收仍成長 26%，但資本支出已超過營業現金流，顯示即使
AI 技術有助於改善廣告推薦及營收變現，龐大基礎建設投入仍可能使短期自由現金流

承壓。不過，資料中心及運算設備通常具有多年使用年限，不能僅以單季現金流評估
整體投資成敗。真正關鍵在於未來 AI 服務收入、營業利益及現金流成長，能否逐步抵
銷前期資本投入。具備明確商業化模式、較高資本效率及穩定現金流的企業，後續表
現可望優於高度依賴融資與成長預期的業者。
4. AI 融資風險仍集中於部分企業 ， 尚不足以認定信用危機全面擴散 ：Meta Q3 財報顯示
，該公司持有 824.5 億美元現金、約當現金及有價證券，顯示其具備一定財務緩衝能
力；相較之下，新興AI 雲端業者因資料中心建置成本龐大、現金流尚未成熟，對信用
市場與外部融資條件的敏感度相對較高 。雖然 AI 資本支出引發投資人重新評估企業信
用風險，但我們認為目前仍缺乏足夠證據認定風險已擴散至整體投資級債市場或銀行
體系。然而，後續仍宜密切觀察 AI 相關企業 CDS 與公司債利差變化、再融資能力及
主要金融機構授信條件對於科技類股及整體股債市場的後續影響。
5. 股價修正不代表企業獲利同步下修，財報表現仍有機會支撐科技股：儘管近期半導體

及 AI 相關股價回落，但台積電與Lam Research 最新財報顯示，部分供應鏈企業營收
及獲利仍維持高度成長，反映股票價格與企業基本面短期可能出現明顯落差。當市場
因融資及資本支出疑慮而降低評價倍數時，即使企業獲利持續增加，股價也可能暫時
承壓。然而，若後續大型科技公司公布的雲端營收、AI 訂單與獲利展望持續優於預期
，企業 EPS 上修仍有機會抵銷部分本益比壓縮壓力。因此，目前尚不宜將科技股價格
修正直接等同於 AI 產業景氣反轉，企業獲利能否持續兌現，仍是中期市場方向的重要
支撐。

策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
6. Fed 與大型科技股財報進入驗證期，短線震盪可能延續：Fed 將於 10 月 27～28 日舉
行利率決策會議，市場除關注是否調整政策利率外，更應重視會後聲明及主席記者會
對通膨、油價與後續利率路徑的態度。另一方面，大型雲端服務商後續公布的財報，
將提供 AI 資本支出、雲端營收及投資效率的最新證據。短線科技股仍可能受到市場評
價偏高、融資風險及地緣政治因素影響而維持震盪；不過，只要企業獲利成長與 AI
需求未出現結構性反轉，中期科技產業基本面仍具支撐，現階段維持短線審慎、中期
偏正向的看法。
四、市場展望：

1. 科技股短線宜審慎觀察 ， 中期仍看好具基本面支撐的AI 產業 ：近期科技股受到 AI 資本支
出、融資能力與評價調整等因素影響，短線波動可能延續，尤其高度依賴外部資金及獲利
尚未成熟的 AI 基礎建設業者，仍面臨較大不確定性。然而，台積電及 Lam Research 最
新財報顯示，先進製程與半導體設備需求尚未出現全面轉弱。我們認為，科技股中期展望
仍偏正向，但市場將更重視企業營收成長、訂單能見度、自由現金流及資本效率。相較缺
乏穩定獲利的 AI 概念企業，具技術優勢、財務結構穩健及獲利成長能見度的科技企業，
風險報酬相對有利。
2. AI 產業由全面成長轉向基本面分化，現金流將成為選股關鍵：隨著大型科技企業持續擴
大 AI 基礎建設投資，市場對未來投資報酬率的要求將同步提高。就目前已公布的科技股
財報結果來看，AI 產業長期成長趨勢尚未改變，但後續股價表現將更加分化，具備穩定
現金流、明確訂單及商業化能力的企業，相對有利；反之，對於高度依賴外部融資、自由
現金流持續為負且缺乏明確獲利時程的企業，仍宜維持審慎。

3. 美債避險功能重現，中天期債券風險報酬相對均衡：半導體類股 10/22 重挫，美國公債
價格上揚、10 年期殖利率盤中跌破 5%，顯示股市風險偏好下降時，美債仍可提供一定
避險效果。雖然 Fed 政策路徑及能源價格仍存在不確定性，但預期相較受政策利率影響
較大的短天期公債，以及受財政赤字、國債供給與期限溢價影響的超長天期公債，中天期
美國公債的風險報酬仍相對較為均衡 。 投資級債因企業信用基本面整體仍具韌性 ， 中期維
持偏正向看法；高收益債則須留意 AI 相關發行人信用風險上升及利差擴大的影響。

Realtek (2379.TW)

Realtek
Company description
Founded in 1987, Realtek is a leading fabless semiconductor with
headquarters in Hsinchu, Taiwan. It has an extensive global footprint with
operational offices in China, Japan, Korea, Singapore, and the US. Realtek's
product portfolio is well-diversified; generally, it can be categorized into three
business groups: Communications Network (Wi-Fi, Bluetooth, automotive
Ethernet, PC Ethernet, switch, etc.), Computer Peripherals (audio codec,

clock IC, card reader, IP cam SoC, USB  hub controller, Type-C controller, SSD
controller) and Multimedia (TV SoC, LCD controller, STB SoC, etc.).
Investment strategy
We rate the shares Buy. We believe Realtek is well-positioned to ride on
continued specs upgrade demand for WiFi, switch and 10G PON as well as the
strong advancements in the automotive and Io T sectors. In the long term, we
believe Realtek's cost-effective, high-performance, and comprehensive wired

and wireless solutions should become more critical with the growing
connectivity requirement at the edge.
Valuation
Our target price for Realtek is set at NT$900 based on our target PE multiple
of 20x to our 2027 EPS. We believe our PER of 20x, higher than its 3-year
forward PER average, is justified by its solid growth opportunity in automotive
and switch/PON business. We also believe its continued efforts in products
mix optimization should help to support its margins expansion. At our target

price, the shares would trade at a 2026/27E P/B of 8x/6x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) Slowdown in technology
migration for its networking business; 2) Weaker-than-expected growth
momentum for automotive Ethernet sales; 3) Worse-than-expected PC/NB
and consumer electronic demand; 4) Slower-than-expected GM expansion
due to pricing competition from peers; and 5) Higher-than-expected memory
cost burden to dampen the order outlook from its networking and PC/NB

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

M
Idea
VSMC could cause  high-single digit (%) dilution to 2027 GM – above many
investors’ 3-5% expectation: On Vanguard’s latest earnings call, management noted
a successful trial run of VisionPower Semiconductor Manufacturing Company, its JV
with NXP Semiconductors. It confirmed that interposers are on track for mass
production  in 1Q27. We see revenue upside from the ramp – but depreciation could
heavily pressure margins. Management previously guided that VSMC could break

even at 30kwpm, more likely in late 2028, in our view (vs. current 8-inch margin at
30-35%). VSMC  capacity is scheduled at 44kwpm, ~100kwpm 8-inch equivalent,
about one-third of current 8-inch capacity (280kwpm). On the other hand, we think
new capacity may only run at <5kwpm in 1Q27 and <20kwpm by end-2027;
depreciation reflects 44kwpm (VSMC's full capacity) for infrastructure, clean room,
and construction. We therefore project that VSMC's gross margin dilution in full-

year 2027 could be more severe than many expect.
Despite strong customer commitment, VSMC could still dilute the corporate
margin average for at least the next five years: Since management previously
guided that VSMC could break even at 30kwpm, we view its break-even point as late
2028 or 1H29. It will then take years for GM to rise from 0% to 30-40%, even if the
capacity is fully booked. We thus expect a drag on corporate average GM for at least

the next five years. The good news: VSMC demand is skewed to high-margin
businesses –  interposers, industrial, and automotive – so the margin improvement
could be smoother  vs. peers like Hua Hong.
2027 might bring double-digit wafer price hikes and AI-related revenue at 20-
30% of the mix: During its latest earnings call, UMC guided for a 10% blended
wafer ASP increase in 2027. Given larger exposure to AI server PMIC, industrial, and
automotive, we expect Vanguard to raise 8-inch wafer prices 10-15% for the full year.

Given some customer pushback to front-loaded hikes, the price hike might be split –
twice a year, each 5-10%. Also, the company would still have L TAs expiring in 2027,
so we don’t expect aggressive wafer ASP guidance for 1Q27. With new interposer
production, we believe AI-related revenue could reach 20-30% of total revenue.
Down to UW from EW: The margin impact is larger than we initially expected and
could last 5-10 years.  Our new price target implies 2.8x our 2027e BVPS.

Vanguard could still show strong revenue/margin
performance for 2H26
4Q26 should maintain strong growth: We expect Vanguard to sustain strong
performance into 4Q26, supported by resilient demand from the industrial and
automotive end markets. In addition to a favorable demand outlook, ongoing capacity
optimization initiatives, particularly the transition from thicker-wire to thinner-wire
manufacturing processes, should enhance production efficiency, improve capacity

utilization, and support higher output. These operational improvements, coupled with a
favorable product mix and steady order momentum, could further underpin revenue
growth and profitability in 4Q26. We now expect Vanguard 8-inch process margin to reach
34-36% in both 4Q26 and 1Q27.

Key questions to ask during the upcoming earnings call on November 6:
1.  Price hike into 2027: How much of the incremental cost could Vanguard pass
through  to customers? Could we see double-digit apples-to-apples price hikes into
2027 despite the expiration of some L TAs?

2.  AI-related revenue: How much could  AI related revenue grow in 2027 if we
include both AI server PMIC and interposer production?
3.  Singapore fab – margin dilution and capacity allocation: How could  margin be
affected by VSMC in both 2027 and 2028? How much would depreciation be in
2027 and 2028? How would the company allocate 12-inch capacity between
interposers, NXP, and other customers?
4.  Consumer demand: Has the company seen any slowdown in consumer demand?

And how soon could that capacity be filled up by other sources of demand?
5.  Further capacity expansion: Might the company have further plans to expand 8-
inch capacity or second Singapore fab since the utilization rate has been reaching
>90%?

Valuation  methodology
We lower our price target (base case value) to NT$135 from NT$180:  We factor in our
earnings estimate changes for 2027-28 and also lower the intermediate growth rate
assumption from 13% to 10.5% to reflect the long-term margin dilution.
We continue to derive our price target from a residual income model, which we believe
best captures the stock's long-term value. Our key assumptions other than the
intermediate growth rate are unchanged, including a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of 1.2,

equity risk premium of 6% and risk-free rate of 2%),  and a terminal growth rate of 4.0%.
We also lower our bull and bear case values from NT$260 and NT$110 to NT$195 and NT
$80, accordingly, implying 4.0x and 1.7x our 2027e BVPS.

MS Rating
▪ We expect 2026 capex to increase beyond
2025's high level given Singapore fab

expansion, leading to long-term margin
pressure.
▪ We expect PMIC wafer price hikes in the
industry in response to strong demand from
AI. Vanguard could beneﬁt from TSMC
mature node consolidation as well as
interposer production.
▪ The new Singapore fab could cause high-
single-digit margin dilution to the corporate
average in 2027.
▪ VSMC's 12-inch capacity is all fully booked;
production will start to ramp in 2027 with
depreciation pressure during the early stage.

▪ Our UW rating is mostly because of the
margin pressure.

NT$195.00
4.0x base case 2027e BVPS
Healthy semi demand: Auto semi demand is
exceptionally strong; inventory is digested
quickly, and DDI for smartphones and large

panels shows robust growth. We forecast a
>30% revenue CAGR in 2025-28.
Competition from peers eases. We assume:
1) global semi sales growth of >20% and
foundry industry growth of >15% in 2026; 2)
better-than-expected demand for 8"
capacity; and 3) power demand for 5G
tracking ahead of schedule.
NT$135.00
2.8x base case 2027e BVPS
Semi growth slowdown: Demand has
started to pick up for LDDIC and PMIC,
while auto semis inventory digestion is
reaching an end. We forecast a 25% revenue

CAGR in 2025-28. Vanguard starts to
reverse pricing discounts to customers in
view of stronger demand and some rush
orders in the short run. We assume: 1) global
semi sales show growth in 2026; and 2) the
foundry industry shows single-digit growth
in 2026.
NT$80.00
1.7x base case 2027e BVPS
Semi growth decelerates: Auto semi demand
weakens markedly; power semi inventory
levels remain elevated. Smartphone DDI
shows a worse-than-expected decline.
Intensiﬁed competition and new Chinese

capacity dampen the overall pricing
environment. We forecast <10% revenue
CAGR in 2025-28. We assume: 1) overall
semi industry continues to decelerate in
2026 and foundry growth is stagnant; and 2)
demand for 8" capacity is weaker than
expected owing to faster product migration.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Earnings revision: We factor in Landmark 3Q26 result and revise up earnings by 9%/ 
3%/ 2%/ 1% in 2026-29E mainly on higher revenues of SiPh CW lasers, supported by 
strong client demand and capacity expansion. We revise up GM by 1ppts in 2026E on 
mix upgrade, and revise down Opex ratio in 2026-29E to reflect improving operational 
efficiency amid the larger scale.

Valuation: We employ a discounted P/E methodology (unchanged) and apply a 61.5x 
(unchanged, report link) target P/E multiple on our 2029E EPS, discounted back to 
2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, risk free rate 1.6%, and market risk premium at 
5.1% - unchanged). Our target P/E multiple of 61.5x is derived from the PEG&M ratio of 
peers (unchanged) in the photonics supply chain. Consequently, our 12-month target 
price is now NT$4,490 (vs. previously at NT$4,459). Our new TP implied 2028E P/E of 
75x is between company’s avg. P/E at 62x and avg. +1 P/E at 91x. Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - LandMark 
Price Target Risks and Methodology - LandMark Optoelectronics Corp.  
Valuation: We employ a discounted P/E methodology, applying a 61.5x target P/E 
multiple on our 2029E EPS, then discount it back to 2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, 
risk free rate 1.6% and market risk premium at 5.1%), to derive our 12-month target 
price of NT$4,490. Our target P/E multiple is based on the same PEG&M ratio as peers in 
the photonics supply chain. We are Buy-rated on LandMark. 
Key risk: Slower-than-expected SiPh penetration; stronger-than-expected competition; 
Integrated Device Manufacturers (IDMs) offering Epitaxial (EPI) wafers in-house.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

### Page 1

WT Microelectronics

Datacenter continues to fuel growth amid 
a less favorable funding surrounding

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 270.00 TWD | Price: 195.00 TWD

3Q26 ahead on operating leverage  
3Q26 net income NT$10.3bn was 4% ahead of BofAe/consensus, thanks mainly to a 
slightly stronger-than-expected operating leverage. By end markets, the sequential 
revenue momentum turned out to be stronger-than-expected across auto, industrial, CE 
(consumer electronics), PC (personal computer) and smartphone verticals in 3Q26, due 
partly to the earlier pull-in which also will likely lead to a QoQ revenue drop in 4Q26 for 
these segments.  
Reaccelerating datacenter business into 4Q26 and 2027 
4Q26 revenue is guided to grow by 25+% QoQ based on the mid-point of guidance 
range, thanks mainly to the reacceleration in datacenter-related business, which more 
than offset the QoQ revenue decline in the segments mentioned above. Yet we expect 
the firm’s interest expense to notably increase in 4Q26 (vs 3Q26) to NT$3bn+, while 
management also attributes such increase to a higher funding cost (interest rate) 
environment and a bigger amount of working capital required to fuel future business 
growth. Looking into 2027, WT Micro expects continuous growth across datacenter, auto 
and industrial areas.   
Lower PO on a lowered multiple given higher interest rate 
We raise 2026-28E EPS by 7-11% after baking in the assumptions of stronger operating 
leverage post the release of 3Q26/4Q26 results/guidance, while our PO goes to NT$270 
(from NT$330) now based on 7x (was 10x) 2H27-1H28E P/E, at the low-to-mid-end of 
its historical 2011-25 trading range of 4-15x, we believe this level of PE is warranted 
because of a higher funding cost (interest rate at 4+%) environment into 2027-28 
balanced by a growing op margin, ROE and EPS CAGR over the forecast period. Given 
the still undemanding valuation at 6x/5x P/E and 11%/14% dividend yield in 2027/28E, 
we reiterate Buy rating on the stock, while staying confident for the company to record 
20+% top-line/bottom-line CAGR during 2026-28E.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
WT Microelectronics was founded in 1993 and went listed 
in 2002. The firm is the biggest distributor for 
semiconductor and technology hardware components 
globally with 10+% market share, and with more than 25k 
customers and over 400 suppliers. WT Micro operates in 
dual-headquarter business model, after acquiring Future 
Electronics in 2024, and now it has over eight thousand 
employees with over 160 sales branches.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on WT Micro, as we like the structural 
growth in datacenter business mainly driven by TPU, as well 
as the emerging benefits/synergies from the cross-selling 
with Future Electronics.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
WT Microelectronics (XZOPF) 
We base our PO NT$270 on 7x 2H27-1H28E P/E, which is at the low-to-mid-end of its 
historical 2011-25 trading range of 4-15x. This is justified in our view, given the higher 
interest rate balanced by the support from: 1) an operating margin of 2.2-2.3% in 2027-
28E, 2) improving ROE toward 33% in 2027-28E (vs 12% in 2024-25), and 3) 22% EPS 
CAGR during 2026-28E.

Upside risks to our PO are: 
1) faster-than-expected demand recovery in auto/industrial verticals, especially from 
US/Europe market 
2) a lower interest rate that could alleviate the company's financial burden at non-
operating level.

Downside risks to our PO are: 
1) a revenue decline in datacenter/server related business, which has been the key 
growth driver during 2021-24 
2) market share loss as suppliers or customers seek other distributors' help after WT 
Micro consolidates Future Electronics. 
3) Potential overhang from share dilution.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 -  Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation). 
Investment rating Total return expectation (within 12-month period of date of initial

INCOME RATINGS, indicators of potential cash dividends, are: 7 - same/higher (dividend considered to be secure), 8 - same/lower (dividend not considered to be 
secure) and 9 - pays no cash dividend.  Coverage Cluster is comprised of stocks covered by a single analyst or two or more analysts sharing a common industry, 
sector, region or other classification(s). A stock’ s coverage cluster is included in the most recent BofA Global Research report referencing the stock.

### Page 2

公司簡介：富世達成立於民國 90 年，母公司為上市公司奇鋐(3017 TT)，公司提供
各類金屬沖壓設計與製造，主要產品為摺疊手機、筆記型電腦、平板電腦之轉軸零
件，近年跨入伺服器領域。3Q26 營收比重：摺疊手機鉸鍊約佔 15.5%，SERVER
相關約佔 81.3%。  
3Q26 本業略優於預期：3Q26 營收 45.02 億(+20.4%QoQ、+26.2%YoY)，受惠
於產品組合、製程改善與自動化導入，3Q26 毛利率達 38%，表現優於預期，營業
利益 14.6 億元，稅後 EPS 18.12 元。 
4Q26 展望持續正向：展望 4Q26 雖然摺疊手機部分，因為記憶體缺貨，終端產品
需求不佳等因素出貨不如預期，但隨著 NVIDIA VR200 與各大專案陸續放量，公
司對整體營運持續維持正向看法 ， 預期伺服器相關出貨將持續增加且營收佔比預期
將維持在高檔； 同時受惠於高毛利的伺服器產品比重提升、製程持續優化與自動化
導入，整體毛利率可望獲得持續改善。預估4Q26 營收 49.66 億元(+10.3%QoQ，
+24.88%YoY)，毛利率 38.15%，稅後 EPS 19.65 元。 
2027 年~2028 年成長動能不虞匱乏：展望 2027 年主要成長動能包括：(1) 預期
對 AWS 的出貨將於 1Q27 開始明顯增溫，Meta、Google、微軟等 CSP 大廠皆於
其 AI 伺服器導入水冷方案 ， 出貨量可望於2027 年放大且貢獻全年營收 ；(2) VR200
已開始出貨，除了浮動接頭持續出貨外，水冷接頭應用由 switch tray 延伸至
computer tray 與 manifold，且營收貢獻延續至 2027 年全年。(3) Vera Rubin 
Ultra swith tray 內 QD 用量由 4 顆變 8 顆，computer tray 相關有 10 顆 MQD
改為 ZQD 有利 ASP 提升(預期較目前 ASP 高一倍以上)，研究處評估整體 Vera 
Rubin ultra 水冷頭產值將較 Vera Rubin 進一步提升 50%以上，順利的將可於
2H27 開始貢獻營收，且貢獻度延伸至 2028 年。 
持續擴充產能：3Q2Q 水冷快接頭產能利用率達 80%~90%，而新產能目前已經
順利開出，產能由先前的3KK 擴增至 4.5KK，預期 2027 年仍將有擴產規劃；滑軌
產能目前維持在先前規劃的 350K 套的水準 ， 後續將視專案認證進度動態規劃產能
擴充。 
研究部預估 2026 年營收 174 億元(+40%YoY)，毛利率 35.8%，稅後 EPS 63.51
元。(前次預估 2026 稅後 EPS 61.86 元)。 預估2027 年營收 245 億元(+41%YoY)，

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
좋은 실적이 상수인 구간 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다. 이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 
이전 전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 26 년 3분기 
영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 상회한 
수준이다. 
실적이 끌고, 수급이 밀고 
지금까지는 높은 실적에 대한 불안보다는 먼 미래에 대한 불확실성을 앞서 주가에 
반영시키는 힘이 더 강하게 작용하다가, 주가가 1,190,000원을 찍은 이후 상승세를 
보이고 있다. 실적을 발표한 10월 28일도 10% 넘게 상승했다. 기대 이상의 실적과 
미국 반도체 시장 분위기가 좋았던 영향이다. 앞으로는 악재에 대해서 민감도가 떨
어지는 구간에 진입할 것으로 전망한다. 지나치게 하락한 주가와 밸류에이션이 버팀
목이 될 것이고, Anthropic의 IPO까지 발표되는 Big Tech 기업들의 실적은 시장의 
우려를 충분히 상쇄할 수 있을 것으로 기대한다. 추가로 진행되는 자사주 매수도 불
안한 수급을 해소할 수 있을 전망이다. 
목표주가는 괴리율을 고려해서 하향 조정 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 26년에도 AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 
시장은 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 
때문이다. 26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 
판단한다. 목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 
업황에 대한 우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 27년 EPS 
538,187원에 PER 6배를 적용하였다.

### Page 2

SK하이닉스 (000660)

2 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 3분기, 모든 악재를 잠재운 실적 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다.  이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 이전 
전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 
DRAM 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 35.4% 증가했다. B/G 는 +11.5%, ASP 는 
+28.2%로 추정했다. 3분기 DRAM 가격 상승률은 경쟁사 대비 높은 수준이다. Server 
DRAM 비중이 2분기 대비 높아진 것과 고객 믹스가 개선된 영향으로 추정한다. 
HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정한다. HBM이 3분기 가격에 미친 영향은 
제한적인 수준으로 판단한다.  
NAND 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 18.2% 증가했다. B/G 는 -1%, ASP는 +26%로 
추정한다. 이전 전망치를 상회했다. SSD 비중은 2분기 75%에서 3분기에는 77%로 
상승했다.  
26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 
상회한 수준이다. DRAM 영업이익은 지난 분기 대비 42.3% 증가한 65.1조원으로 
예상한다. 영업이익률은 전 분기 대비 4.0%p 상승한 83.0%로 추정한다. NAND 
영업이익은 전 분기 대비 23.1% 증가한 18.2조원으로 추정한다. 영업이익률은 전 
분기 대비 2.9%p 높아진 72.4%로 추정한다.

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 4분기, DRAM 물량 효과 
SK하이닉스의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 29.4% 증가한 135.3조원으로 
예상한다. 가격상승은 지속될 것으로 예상하지만 26년 3분기 대비 상승폭이 낮아질 
전망이다. 물량은 3분기 대비 25% 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 신규 라인 가동 영향이다. 
4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 34.1% 증가한 111.7조원으로 예상한다. DRAM 
90.9조원, NAND 20.7조원이다.  
DRAM 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 36.4% 증가한 107조원으로 예상한다. ASP 는 
+15%, Bit Growth는 +25.0%로 가정하였다. HBM 출하는 지난 분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 예상하고, HBM4 비중은 60%대로 높아질 전망이다. 
26년 4분기 NAND 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 11.6% 증가할 것으로 예상한다. Bit 
Growth는 +5%로 전망하고, ASP는 +12%로 예상한다. SSD 비중은 소폭 개선될 
전망이다. SSD 내에서 eSSD 비중은 90% 수준이다.

IBKS RESEARCH │5 
공정 전환 속도는 예상보다 빠르게 진행 중: 1cb nm, 321L 
SK하이닉스는 1c nm 비중을 빠르게 확대하고 있다. Wafer 투입이 제한된 상황에서 
공급 확대를 위한 최선의 방법이기 때문이다. HBM 수요는 1b nm를 중심으로 대응할 
예정이다. 이와 관련된 물량도 꾸준히 증가하지만, 출하 Bit 기준으로 1c nm 비중이 
26년 1분기 3% 수준에서 26년 3분기에는 20%, 연말에는 33%까지 높아질 전망이다. 
HBM이 아닌 DRAM 물량이 크게 증가할 전망이다. 이를 통해서 높아진 DRAM 수요 
대응할 것으로 예상한다. 26년 출하 Bit Growth는 28.5%로 상향되었다. 
NAND 생산라인의 공정 전환은 High-End로 꾸준히 변화 중이다. 321L 는 26년 
2분기에는 176L와 Cross over 되었다. 예상보다 1분기 빠른 속도다. 26년 3분기 321L 
비중은 50%이다. 26년말까지 321L를 64%까지 확대할 계획이다.

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
Check Point 
1. DRAM ASP의 차별화 
26년 3분기 DRAM ASP는 메이커별로 차별화가 크다. 26년 2분기 대비 상승폭은 마
이크론이 10% 후반, 삼성전자는 20% 초반으로 추정하고, SK하이닉스는 20% 후반이
다. LTA, HBM 등 다양한 변수가 있지만 SK하이닉스는 고객 비중 변화가 가장 큰 변
수라고 설명한다.  
2. HBM4 물량, 수율 문제 
26년 3분기에 가장 시끄러웠던 변수가 SK하이닉스의 HBM4 관련 품질 문제이다. 26
년 3분기 DRAM 매출과 영업이익으로는 문제가 있었다고 판단하기 어렵다. 26년 3분
기 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정되고 4분기 말에는 비중이 60%
까지 높아질 것으로 전망한다.   
3. HBM 8Hi 확대가 시장 규모 감소는 아니다. 
HBM4는 12Hi가 원래 제품 사양이다. HBM 공급이 부족해지면서 GPU를 좀 더 많이 
팔기 위해서 적용되는 HBM 용량을 줄이는 방향으로 제품 설계가 변경되는 중이다. 
2027년 하반기에는 8Hi 비중이 12Hi 비중을 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 하지만 이로 
인해서 HBM 전체 물량이 감소하는 것은 아닌 것으로 전망하고 있다.  
4. 투자와 생산은 다르다 
SK하이닉스는 용인팹을 공기를 앞당겨서 완공할 계획이다. 이로 인해서 공급량이 예
상 대비 커지는 것에 대한 우려가 있지만 건물 완공과 장비 반입에는 시차가 있고, 수
요를 고려하지 않은 장비 반입은 없을 것으로 밝혔다. 
5. 주주환원. 더 남아 있다 
공시를 통해서 26년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원의 주주환원 계획을 밝혔다. 배당을 30
조원 이상, 자사주 매입/소각을 60조원이상을 추진할 계획이다. 26년 4분기 중에 배
당은 14조원을 집행하고, 자사주는 20조원을 매입할 예정이다. 자사주 20조원 중에서 
10조원은 종업원 상여금 지급용이어서 주주환원에 포함되지 않는다.  
26년 4분기 실적 발표에서 추가적인 배당과 자사주 매입에 대한 계획을 발표할 것으
로 예상한다.

ASP 움직임은 메이커별로

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
펀더멘탈과 센티멘탈의 줄다리기 구간 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 시장은 
성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 
26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 판단한다.  
목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 업황에 대한 
우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 2027년 EPS 538,187원에 PER 
6배를 적용하였다.  
아직 제대로 보급되지 않은 Agent AI가 확산되면 컴퓨팅 파워 필요성에 대한 인식도 
크게 바뀔 것으로 전망한다. 메타의 MUSE는 가입자당 8GB의 DRAM을 가정하고 
있다. Open AI, Google, Anthropic 에서도 Agent Platform이 공개된다면 필요한 
DRAM은 폭발적으로 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 
메모리 수요에 대한 규모가 너무 커지는 것이 걱정이지 누가 살 것인지에 대한 것은 
걱정할 문제가 아니라 판단한다. 기존 AI 모델의 진화, 효율적 모델의 등장, Agent AI 
모델의 확대 모두 메모리 수요를 기하급수적으로 확장시키는 변수이다. 
투자 주체들의 자금난이 수요를 저해하는 변수는 아닌 것으로 판단한다. 대체 
투자자들은 지속적으로 증가할 것으로 전망하기 때문이다.  
메모리 업체들의 투자 계획은 수요에 대응하기 위한 투자일 뿐 공급과잉을 만들 
수준은 아닐 것으로 판단한다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

2026.10.30   
한화에어로스페이스  
(012450)

3Q26: 그래도 믿는다  Company Brief

3분기 매출 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원 
한화에어로스페이스의 3분기 매출은 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원(OPM 7.4%)로 
컨센서스와 당사 추정치를 모두 대폭 하회하는 실적을 기록하였다. 앞서 실적을 발표한 
한화오션의 실적 쇼크를 감안하더라도, 주력사업인 지상방산 부문의 실적이 매출 1조 
2,455억원/영업이익 217억원(1.7%)로 지난 분기(2조 1,075억원/ 5,330억원/ 25.3%) 
는 물론 전년동기(2조 1,098억원/5,725억원/27.1%)보다도 현저히 감소하였다. 
일회성 요인은 대전사업장 폭발사고에 따른 복구비용 등 300억원, 일부 국내사업 
충당금 160억원 및 환율하락에 따른 효과 등이 있었으나 역시 이를 감안해도 내수와 
수출 모두 실질 이익률은 5%미만으로 추정된다. 지상방산 분기 매출은 1분기(1조 
2,211억)보다 오히려 소폭 증가하였으나 영업이익이 1분기 2,087억원에서 
217억원으로 대폭 감소한 점에 대하여 회사는 프로젝트별 믹스 구성에 따른 낮은 
이익률의 인도분이 반영되었다고 설명하고 있으나, 그럼에도 불구하고 수출과 내수 
이익률 모두 BEP에 가까웠다는 점은 쉽게 이해가 가지는 않는다. 다만 방산기업의 
특성상 대전사업장 사고에 따른 실제 납기 영향 등 회사가 공개할 수 없는 사정이 
있었을 것으로 추측할 뿐이다. 대전사업장은 현재 복구작업이 진행 중이며 27년 
상반기 중에는 가동 정상화할 것으로 예상된다.  
긍정적인 소식도 있다. 폴란드향 천무의 연간 인도대수가 기존 가이던스인 40대에서 
60대로 증가하였으며, 이를 포함하여 3분기에 인도되지 않은 물량의 대부분이 
4분기에 집중될 예정으로, 올해의 4분기 실적 개선폭은 통상적으로 볼 수 있는 
4분기의 계절적 집중도보다 더 커질 것으로 예상한다. 방산기업의 실적은 인도일정에 
따라 크게 변할 수 있는 분기 실적보다 연간 합산이 더 중요하다는 점을 한 번 더 
믿고, 4분기를 기다리는 게 좋겠다.

투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 1,200,000원 하향  
투자의견 BUY를 유지하지만, SOTP기반 목표주가는 기존 1,490,000원에서 
1,200,000원으로 19.5% 하향한다. 비영업가치 중 자회사 한화시스템과 한화오션, 
쎄트렉아이의 지분가치 하락(합산 8.7조원→7.1조원), 그리고 지난 분기 대비 늘어난 
별도 순차입금(3.25조→4.95조원)을 반영하였으며 최근 방산섹터 전반의 밸류 하락을 
감안하여 영업가치 중 지상방산+항공우주의 Target P/E를 기존 35배에서 30배로 
조정하였다. 
3분기는 여러모로 악재가 겹쳤다. 주식시장 전반의 하락에 더해 방산섹터 수주 지연에 
따른 디레이팅, 그리고 납기 지연에 따른 실적 하락 등 나올 수 있는 악재는 다 
나왔다고도 볼 수 있다. 그러나 뒤집어보면 지연중인 수주는 시간의 문제일 뿐 가시화 
가능성이 매우 높으며, 실적 또한 진행률 인식이 아닌 인도기준 인식에 따른 일시적 
현상으로 4분기 이후 복구될 가능성이 크다. 펀더멘털의 유의미한 변화는 없다고 볼 
수 있으며 밸류에이션 매력도가 증가한 현 시점에서 추가 매수를 고려할 때다. 다만, 
대전사업장 사고에 따른 당국의 제재조치가 아직 확정되지 않았음은 잔여 리스크 
요인이다. 동종사의 신규계약 입찰제한 제재조치가 주가에 상당한 영향을 미쳤음을 
고려하면 한화에어로스페이스 또한 제재조치 여부를 지켜본 후 투자할 것을 권한다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
 당 연구원은 한화에어로스페이스(012450)의 일부 비용을 지원받아 한화에어로스페이스 공장 투어(2026년 10월 1-2)를 다녀왔습니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

두산에너빌리티 (034020)

Company Brief 
유틸리티/건설 
Analyst 문경원, CFA 
kyeongwon.moon@meritz.co.kr 
RA 이동석 
dongseok.lee@meritz.co.kr 3Q26 Review: 바닥을 잡는다면 지금  
믹스 개선에 따른 이익률 개선 조금씩 관찰 중 
3Q26 연결 영업이익은 2,612억원(+90.6% YoY) 을 기록하며 컨센서스(2,455억원)
를 소폭 상회했다. 밥캣(1,674억원)이 컨센서스(1,761억원)를 소폭 하회한 가운데, 
에너빌리티 부문 영업이익(+179% YoY, OPM 6.6%) 이 호실적을 이끌었다. 환율 하
락에도 불구, 가스터빈, 원전 등 고수익성 프로젝트의 비중 상승이 주요 원인이다. 
3Q26 누적 수주는 10.0조원(+85.5% YoY) 을 기록했다. 4Q26 가스복합 EPC 수주 
등을 감안하면 연간 가이던스(13.3조원) 달성은 무난하다. 한수원과의 협상 결렬로 
이목이 집중된 체코 두코바니 시공 수주는 2027년 인식될 확률이 높아 보인다. 주간
사가 바뀌더라도 이미 프로젝트에 참여 중인 동사를 배제하기는 쉽지 않다.

대미투자 이후, 연말~연초 수주 모멘텀에 집중 
연말 연초에 다음과 수주 모멘텀이 집중되면서, 주가 상승을 이끌 전망이다. 
1) 대형원전: 정부 자금 중심의 Westinghouse LLI(장납기 기자재) 발주가 몰린다. 
우선 미국 DOE(에너지부)가 제공한 대출을 기반으로 미국 수주가 기대된다.  폴란드 
정부 역시 최근 LLI 발주 작업을 시작했다. 한편, 한국 정부는 연말까지 100억 달러 
규모의 대미 투자 송금을 협의 중인데, 이 역시 DOC(상무부) 주도의 대형 원전 건설
을 위한 LLI 발주에 쓰일 가능성이 높다 
2) SMR: 오래 기다려왔던 Nuscale 향 수주가 시작되고 있다. 우선 29일 모듈 1기 
제작 계약을 체결했다. 현금이 부족한 Nuscale이 용기를 냈다는 건, 곧 Nuscale-
TVA 간 PPA 가 임박했음을 의미한다. 본격적인 수주는 PPA 직후 예상된다.  
3) 가스 터빈: 2026년 초 해외 수주(7기)는 2029년까지 인도 계획이다. 이후 2030
년 슬랏 예약을 완료하고 2031년 물량을 논의 중으로 보인다. 지금은 2030년 슬랏
에 대한 PO가 나와 수주잔고에 인식할 타이밍이다. 역시 수출 물량으로 추측한다.

성장에 대한 기대가 사라진 밸류에이션, 지금이 바닥 
투자의견 Buy를 유지, 할인율 상승을 반영하여 적정주가를 109,000원으로 -17% 
하향 조정한다. 금리 상승, 환율 하락, 트럼프 지지율 하락 등 Top-down 리스크에 
최근 조정을 받았지만, 추가적인 주가 하락 폭은 제한적일 전망이다. EV/선행 수주잔
고 지표는 현재 1.0배 수준까지 하락하며, 원전의 성장성이 주목받지 못했던 
2021~2024년 수준에 근접하게 내려왔다.  
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 30

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 109,000원 
현재주가 (10.29) 56,700원 
상승여력 92.2%

Document 251: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점불확실성보다 성장_3801b631

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점불확실성보다 성장_3801b631.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
3분기 실적 컨센서스 상회 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 매출액은 1조 4,903억 원(+18.5% Y oY), 
영업이익은 6,401억 원(+1.0% Y oY, 영업이익률 43.0%)으로 시장 컨센서스를 각각 
10%, 5% 상회했다. 1~4공장의 풀가동이 이어진 가운데 5공장의 인증용 생산배치 
물량과 미국 락빌 공장 매출(약 1,000억 원)이 처음으로 반영되며 외형 성장을 
견인했다. 매출원가율은 50.4%로 전년 동기(41.4%), 전분기(47.0%) 대비 
상승했는데, 락빌 공장과 5공장의 초기 물량 반영에 따른 것으로 판단한다. 향후 
물량 상승에 따라 매출원가율은 감소할 것으로 예상한다. 그럼에도 EBITDA 
마진율은 50.4%로 높은 수준을 유지하며 7,515억 원을 달성했다. 순이익은 원/달러 
환율 하락에 따른 외화환산손실이 반영되며 3,911억 원을 기록했다.  
환율 하락에도 가이던스 상향하며 성장 궤도 유지 
2026년 연간 매출액은 5조 6,133억 원(+23.2% Y oY), 영업이익은 2조 4,605억 
원(+18.9% YoY)으로 전망한다. 최근 원/달러 환율 하락에도 이번 실적발표에서 
상향 제시한 연간 매출 성장률 20% 이상은 충분히 달성 가능할 것으로 판단한다. 
4분기에는 락빌 공장의 매출 약 1,000억 원과 5공장 가동 확대에 더해, 5월 
파업으로 이연된 물량의 매출 인식이 성장에 기여할 전망이다.  
투자의견 매수 및 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
비만 치료제 CDMO 진출을 위한 폴리펩타이드 그룹 인수는 공개매수 응모율 
87.88%로 최소 조건을 충족해 11월 말 완료를 앞두고 있어 신규 성장 동력은 
확보된 것으로 판단한다. 노사 리스크도 법원의 쟁의행위 제한 확대에 이어 노조가 
2차 총파업을 잠정 연기하며 크게 완화됐다. 미국 관세 불확실성으로 부진했던 신규 
수주 모멘텀은 올해 연말부터 내년에 걸쳐 강화될 것으로 전망하며, 불확실성 
해소와 성장 기대감을 반영할 시점이라 판단한다. 
목표주가 (유지) 2,000,000원

Document 272: AI バブル測定器としての鉱工業生産（26 年９月）

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI バブル測定器としての鉱工業生産（26 年９月）.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 30 日(金)

1/5 
AI バブル測定器としての鉱工業生産（26 年９月） 
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・筆者が日本経済と株式市場の行方を読む上で定点観測している日本の鉱工業生産は、2026年
９月に前月比＋2.2％となり、市場予想（同＋1.3％）を上回った。経産産業省の予測補正値
（同＋1.6％）に対しても上振れた。増産は３ヶ月ぶり。８月が速報段階の同▲1.7％から確
報で同▲2.4％へと下方修正されていたため、９月の増産で前月までの落ち込みを概ね取り戻
した格好となる。もっとも、生産水準は直近最高だった６月の104.6を上回るには至っておら
ず、数量ベースの生産活動は、経済産業省の判断通り「一進一退」となっている。

・日経平均株価は先行き12 ヶ月72,000円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPYは先行き12ヶ月 155円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を12月に 1.5％とした後、27年末までに2.25％とするだろう 
・FEDはFF金利を、年内は4.0％で据え置くだろう

### Page 2

2/5 
・増産となったのは８業種。上位３業種は自動車工業、生産用機械工業、汎用・業務機械工業
であった。反対に減産の寄与度が大きかった３業種は鉄鋼・非鉄金属、窯業・土石製品工
業、化学工業（除く無機・有機化学工業・医薬品）であった。７月に熊本で発生した地震に
よって８月までは自動車生産などに影響が出ていたが、その挽回生産が９月の数値を押し上
げる格好となった。

・10月初旬に実施された生産予測調査に基づけば、製造工業の生産計画は10月に前月比＋5.1％
となった後、11月は同＋0.8％と増産が見込まれている。経産省経済解析室が生産予測指数の
上振れバイアスを補正した10月の予測値は同＋3.9％とかなり強い。電子部品・デバイス工業
と生産用機械工業で大幅な増産が見込まれているほか、自動車の挽回生産も続く見込みであ
る。生産活動全体の方向感を決める輸送機械工業は９月（実績）に前月比＋5.5％となった
後、10月（計画）に同＋0.9％、11月に同＋4.3％となっている。日本や米国における自動車
販売台数が堅調に推移していることを踏まえると、基調的に弱含んでいく展開は想定しにく
い。また生産活動の先行指標として有用な製造業ＰＭＩが４月以降に有意な上昇を示してい
ることから判断すると、製造業全体としてはＡＩ・半導体需要が浸透する形で増勢が強まっ
ていくと期待される。10月製造業ＰＭＩは53.4と高水準を維持している。

・なお、鉱工業生産指数は生産の「数量」に重きを置いて生産高を計測する統計であり、「付
加価値」を直接捕捉する性格のものではない。例えば、高付加価値を狙った品質向上によっ
て生産数量・重量が減少する場合、統計上はそれを減産と見做し、付加価値を過小評価して
しまうこともある。したがって、鉱工業生産統計が示すほど日本経済（製造業）の付加価値

3/5 
創出力が停滞していない可能性を念頭に置く必要がある。事実、過去数年は付加価値の合計
である実質ＧＤＰと鉱工業生産指数の水準・方向感の乖離が大きくなっている。

・株式市場における重要度が高い電子部品・デバイス工業については、前月比＋3.6％の増産で
あった。地震の影響もあってか、ＩＣの増産に一服感がみられる一方、コンデンサや電子回
路基板などで増産の動きがみられる。生産用機械工業に分類される半導体製造装置は同＋
3.4％の増産となり、３ヶ月平均値でみても＋7.7％と力強い増産基調にある。生産予測調査
に目を向けると、電子部品・デバイス工業は10月に同＋16.6％、11月に同▲1.7％と均してみ
れば大幅な増産が計画されている。半導体製造装置が含まれる生産用機械工業は10月に同＋
15.5％と大幅増産となった後、11月は同▲5.7％と減産が見込まれているが、こちらも基調は
強い。個別企業の業績予想などから判断しても、先行きは半導体製造装置の増産がかなりの
高確度で期待できる。関連指標の機械受注統計における「電子計算機等」の受注額水準も鈍
化する気配に乏しい。この指標は日経平均株価との連動性がある。

### Page 4

4/5

・電子部品・デバイス工業の動向を仔細にみると、９月は出荷が前年比＋0.8％へと減速するな
か、在庫は同▲6.1％へとマイナス幅を拡大した。この結果、出荷・在庫バランス（両者の前
年比差分から算出）は＋6.9pとなり、３ヶ月平均でも＋7.0ptとはっきりとしたプラス領域を
維持した。引き続き、製品需給が引き締まる方向にあることが示唆されている。ここで電子
部品・デバイス工業の出荷・在庫バランスと、日経平均株価が長期的に連動性を有してきた
事実を再確認したい。株価指数において半導体を直接手掛ける企業の存在感は必ずしも大き
くはないものの、半導体製造装置、電子部品、化学品など「広義半導体」で見ればその存在
感は大きく、結果的に日本株全体のうねりを作り出す構図があると筆者は理解している。ち
なみに出荷在庫バランス株価は方向感こそ一致しているものの、両者のかい離は大きくなっ
ている。その要因として、数量ベースの出荷・在庫バランスには、メモリなど製品価格の高
騰が反映されないことが考えられる。

れる。

Document 328: COP31_ Why it still matters for businesses

Source PDF: all report pdf/COP31_ Why it still matters for businesses.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

Is COP still worth following? We think so. What matters for
businesses is whether this year’s COP31 can improve the
business case for action. Here are four areas to watch

Coco Zhang and Gerben Hieminga
The global climate landscape has changed signiﬁcantly since world
leaders committed 11 years ago to limiting global warming to well
below 2°C and pursuing eﬀorts to limit warming to 1.5°C.
Geopolitical tensions, policy shifts, trade protectionism, rising
energy demand… they have all made it harder for governments to
turn climate commitments into action.
That raises an important question for businesses: Is the Conference
of the Parties (COP) still worth following?
Our answer is yes.
Businesses do not just invest in climate targets; they invest in viable
projects and operations. But many of them still face economic and
practical barriers. Grid constraints and high electricity costs can
slow electriﬁcation. Greener industrial products can struggle to

### Page 2

attract buyers willing to pay a premium. And climate adaptation
plans do not always translate into investable projects.
Businesses cannot solve these challenges alone. In a 2025 UN
survey of more than 2,000 CEOs across 128 countries, 92% said
strong global governance and coordinated policy are “critical” or
“important” to implementing their sustainability agendas.
That is why COP is still relevant. It can help align policy priorities,
improving investment conditions, and connecting businesses with

the right resources. At COP31, four areas will be particularly
important for businesses to watch.
Electriﬁcation marries economic growth
with sustainability
Electriﬁcation is increasingly important for economic growth,
energy security, and sustainability. Demand from AI, EVs, heat
pumps and industrial decarbonisation is accelerating electricity
use, which is growing twice as fast as overall energy demand.
Electricity also punches above its weight economically, accounting

for 23% of global ﬁnal energy consumption but powering 46% of
global GDP. This implies that high value-added sectors rely more on
electricity than the overall economy.
Electricity’s economic contribution
outweighs its share of energy use

### Page 3

Source: ING Research, International Energy Agency
COP31’s push to accelerate electriﬁcation reﬂects this trend. The
COP31 presidency has set a goal of increasing electricity’s share of
ﬁnal energy consumption from 23% today to 35% by 2035.
Getting there will require addressing a major constraint: the grid. As
we have written, grid congestion could slow electriﬁcation, increase
costs, and delay electricity-dependent economic activity.
The key question at COP31 is how governments turn this ambition

into action. We think eﬀective action could include:
Establish a detailed electriﬁcation roadmap with interim
benchmarks to guide progress and sustain momentum across
future COPs
Enhance knowledge-sharing platforms. The International
Smart Grid Action Network has extensive experience sharing
best practices, but could go further by translating knowledge
into direct technical assistance and ready-to-deploy models
Set country- or sector-level targets and pathways to encourage

industry alignment and commitment
Improve access to ﬁnance by expanding dedicated ﬁnancing
for developing countries, while lowering upfront costs in Europe
Improve the economics. Coordinate energy pricing reforms to
make electriﬁcation more competitive with fossil fuel
combustion alternatives, including by reducing taxes and grid
fees that raise electricity prices
Strengthen infrastructure by encouraging governments to
reduce permitting barriers (e.g. Europe and the US), advance

### Page 4

regulatory support for power demand ﬂexibility, and expand
cross-border grids where needed (e.g. Southeast Asia)
If COP31 delivers meaningful action in these areas, the implications
for businesses could be signiﬁcant. Stronger policy could accelerate
near-term grid connections and enhance long-term investment
conﬁdence. Meanwhile, companies may need to work more closely
with utilities and regulators to secure the power they need.
Green industrialisation remains critical

But electriﬁcation is only part of the story. Industry is another
major source of process emissions, with sectors such as steel,
cement, and aviation accounting for roughly 20% of the global
total. Cutting these emissions is particularly diﬃcult because many
industrial processes still have few cost-eﬀective alternatives without
lengthy site overhauls.
COP31 has therefore put green industrialisation high on the
agenda, building on COP30’s progress. Following the Belém

Declaration on Global Green Industrialisation at COP30, a coalition
led by the Brazilian and UK governments mobilised hundreds of
millions of dollars in concessional ﬁnance for Brazilian industries
and awarded funding to 12 hydrogen projects. Over time, these
eﬀorts could reshape manufacturing processes and investment.
But many industrial decarbonisation technologies still face
signiﬁcant barriers to scaling. Their high cost premiums mean
economics depend heavily on government support. Demand for

cleaner industrial products remains limited. And the necessary
infrastructure for many is still underdeveloped.
Hard-to-abate sectors’ green premium is
way higher than what companies are willing
to pay

COP31 is not setting a new level of ambition for green
industrialisation. It’s focusing on implementing the existing
agenda. One priority is narrowing the demand gap. COP31 can
coordinate governments to create more demand for low-carbon
industrial products through long-term public procurement. If such
eﬀorts gain momentum at COP31, they could provide the long-run
revenue certainty investors need to back green industrial projects at

scale.
Climate ﬁnance faces multiple gaps
To realise the targets and priorities set forth at COP, investment is
crucial. And total ﬂows are growing. Global climate ﬁnance reached
around $2tn in 2024 and continued to grow in 2025, although at a
slower rate.

Source: Climate Policy Initiative, ING Research
But the headline number masks signiﬁcant gaps. First, even though
climate mitigation (i.e. emissions reduction) ﬁnance accounted for
$1.9tn of the $2tn total, the level falls well short of estimated
needs, which range from $7.6tn-$9.6tn through 2050. In areas such
as electriﬁcation and green industrialisation, funding is needed not
only for technologies, but also for the infrastructure and demand

needed to scale them.
Second, only $64bn of the total went exclusively to adaptation, an
area requiring surging investment because of intensifying climate
risks. Third, a substantial share of climate ﬁnance stays within
advanced economies or comes from domestic sources.
International ﬂows to help developing countries totalled only
$175bn, far below COP29’s target of $1.3tn per year by 2035.
Imbalances among growing global climate
ﬁnance
Finance levels in $bn

### Page 7

Source: ING Research, Climate Policy Initiative, UN, UNEP, Natural
Resources Defense Council
More climate products and solutions are emerging in the latter two
segments. But their higher risks or weaker business cases can make
it diﬃcult to attract capital without supportive policies. Investors are
actively turning to blended ﬁnance, which uses public capital to
help de-risk projects and mobilise private investment. Yet many still
lack the data, resources and connections needed to move projects

forward. This is where COP31 can help. As we discuss in the next
section, it can help governments create more favourable
investment conditions and connect suppliers, buyers and investors.
Adaptation is becoming a business
imperative and opportunity
Business awareness of climate adaptation has grown, as many
businesses are experiencing more disruptions ﬁrsthand. Our
previous analysis also shows how drought on Europe’s Rhine River,
hurricanes in the US, and other extreme weather events are already

aﬀecting supply chains across sectors. In aggregate, physical
climate risks could cost companies in the S&P Global 1200 index
$1.2tn annually by 2050.
One barrier to corporate investment in climate adaptation is that it
is still seen primarily as a way to avoid losses. But adaptation can
also create business value. Investments in resilient assets, cooling
systems, supplier diversiﬁcation and climate-resilient materials can
improve reliability, competitiveness and long-term growth.

Research from the World Resources Institute suggests that every $1
invested in adaptation can generate up to $10 in beneﬁts. That
return could be even higher for highly climate-exposed sectors. For

### Page 8

example, the World Economic Forum estimates returns of $19–$43
per dollar invested in the food and beverage sector.
COP can help turn adaptation priorities into investable projects.
Since the Paris Agreement, countries have developed National
Adaptation Plans (NAPs), but many remain high-level and lack
speciﬁc, actionable projects. Recent COP eﬀorts have sought to
change this by encouraging governments to translate their NAPs
into portfolios of investable adaptation projects.

But identifying projects does not guarantee ﬁnancing. This is where
COP31 could make a diﬀerence. Its proposed Climate
Implementation Bridge (BRIDGE) initiative could make projects
easier for investors to identify and assess by centralising
information on technical requirements, risks, costs and beneﬁts.
Expanding the initiative to more countries could give more banks,
investors and companies access to potential adaptation
investments.
For businesses, a successful COP31 could therefore help unlock

capital for adaptation projects. The beneﬁts could extend
throughout supply chains, as companies increasingly depend on
the climate resilience of their suppliers to maintain reliable
operations.
Conclusion
The most eﬀective corporate sustainability strategies support long-
term growth, but they are more eﬀective with international
coordination and strong policy support. COP can bring
governments and businesses together to provide such a
framework, even amid geopolitical tensions and concerns over

slowing progress. COP31 may not deliver a landmark global
agreement, but practical advances in these four priorities could still
have immediate relevance for businesses.

Document 357: France’s social demands run up against tightening fiscal constraints

Source PDF: all report pdf/France’s social demands run up against tightening fiscal constraints.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 6

### Page 1

Thursday’s nationwide mobilisation over wages and purchasing
power will add pressure on a French government already
struggling with weak growth, a fragile labour market and an
increasingly unmanageable budget. Its options for responding
to social discontent are narrowing as higher borrowing costs
further weaken the outlook for public debt
Charlotte de Montpellier
Student-led protests have grown into a broader social movement, putting
more pressure on the French government

Pressure mounts for action on purchasing
power
What began as a student-led protest has grown into a broader
social movement, adding to the government’s challenges as it
struggles to get its 2027 budget through parliament.
The mobilisation continued during the school holidays, with
between 320,000 and 500,000 people taking to the streets last
Saturday, fewer than in previous weeks but hardly the pause the

### Page 2

government had hoped for. The next test will come on Thursday, 5
November, when all the main trade unions will mobilise over wages
and purchasing power, with students and public sector workers
expected to join the protests.
Under pressure, the government is considering raising the
minimum wage by more than the automatic increase of around
1% due in January, possibly adding another 0.5 to 0.6 percentage
points. This would be the ﬁrst discretionary increase since 2012. But

employers are strongly opposed, as they are already facing higher
labour costs through planned cuts to social contribution
exemptions. A further rise in the minimum wage could therefore
encourage companies to limit recruitment or reduce their
workforce, at a time when the labour market is already fragile.
Private sector employment has declined for seven consecutive
quarters in annual terms and now stands 0.3% lower than a year
ago, representing a loss of almost 70,000 jobs. A discretionary

increase in the minimum wage would also have implications for the
public sector. It could raise the pay of around 15% of public sector
employees, adding further pressure on the public ﬁnances.
Part of the additional wage cost could also be passed on to
consumers, adding to inﬂationary pressures. Higher energy prices
have already pushed headline inﬂation from 3.1% in September to
an estimated 3.4% in October, while harmonised inﬂation reached
3.8%.

Budget talks are producing a larger deﬁcit,
not a solution
These pressures are making an already diﬃcult budget equation
even harder to solve. After 10 full days of debate and more than
2,300 amendments examined, France’s 2027 budget discussions
appear to be going nowhere, except towards a larger deﬁcit than
the government intended. While the government aims to reduce
the deﬁcit to 5% of GDP in 2027, after an expected 5.4% in 2026,
the measures approved so far have pushed it substantially further

oﬀ course. According to the Finance Ministry, the amendments
adopted during the ﬁrst reading have already worsened the

### Page 3

projected deﬁcit by €23.9bn (total deﬁcit in 2026 will be around
€165bn).
The vote on the revenue part has been postponed again, as MPs
continue to adopt measures pointing in all directions. Yet the
exercise increasingly makes little sense: the fragmented National
Assembly is unlikely to approve the budget, leaving the
government with two possible escape routes. It could use Article
49.3 to pass the bill without a vote, but would ﬁrst need to
negotiate with parts of the opposition to ensure that a subsequent

no-conﬁdence motion fails. For now, there are few signs of such
talks. If the motion passed, the government would fall and the
budget would be rejected. Alternatively, once the constitutional 70-
day deadline expires on 18 December, the government could
implement its original budget by ordinance. A two-stage solution,
with the ordinance followed by a corrective bill, is reportedly
gaining support among MPs, including some Socialists. The
corrective bill could accommodate selected social promises.

Weak growth makes the ﬁscal targets
unrealistic
The problem is that the government’s initial budget will no longer
be suﬃcient to bring the deﬁcit down to 5% of GDP. It was based on
GDP growth of 1% in 2027, an assumption that now looks clearly
too optimistic. The French economy contracted by 0.1% in the third
quarter, after stagnating in the second and shrinking by 0.2% in the
ﬁrst, leaving GDP marginally lower than a year earlier and France
hovering close to recession.

The details of the third-quarter ﬁgure were also weak: investment
fell by 0.6% and net trade subtracted 0.6 percentage points from
growth as exports dropped sharply, while inventories provided a
temporary oﬀset. The only real positive surprise came from
household consumption, which rose by 0.4%. But there is little
reason to expect this momentum to last. Consumer conﬁdence fell
by ﬁve points to 80 in October, its lowest level since June 2013 and
well below its long-term average of 100. Households’ assessment

of whether this is a good time to make major purchases dropped

### Page 4

sharply, while fears of unemployment increased. Most strikingly,
their assessment of living standards fell to its lowest level since the
survey began in October 1972. This points to much weaker
consumption in the coming months.
The outlook for investment is equally weak. Financing conditions
are deteriorating particularly sharply in France: according to the
latest ECB Bank Lending Survey, a net 27% of French banks
tightened credit standards for businesses in the third quarter,

compared with around 8% in Germany, no tightening in Spain and
an easing in Italy. Together with weak demand, higher interest
rates and uncertainty over taxes and public spending, this is likely
to encourage companies and households to postpone projects.
Combined with political uncertainty and social tensions, growth is
likely to be very weak in the fourth quarter as well. All in all, we now
expect GDP growth of just 0.3% this year and 0.5% in 2027, with

risks still tilted to the downside. Under these assumptions,
implementing the budget unchanged would leave the deﬁcit at
around 5.5% of GDP. Reaching the government’s 5% target would
require roughly €12bn in additional savings, on top of the €43bn
eﬀort already included in the budget, which looks highly unlikely in
the current political environment. And that is before including the
almost €500mn cost of the measures promised to high-school and
university students.

Debt is unlikely to stabilise anytime soon
All this leaves France’s debt dynamics looking increasingly
uncomfortable. The government had expected public debt to reach
121.7% of GDP in 2027, already a level at which a 5% deﬁcit would
be insuﬃcient to stabilise the debt ratio. With growth now likely to
be weaker and the deﬁcit higher than assumed in the budget, debt
will probably rise even further, and there is little prospect of
stabilisation anytime soon.

The sharp rise in borrowing costs only makes the arithmetic worse.
The French 10-year yield is currently around 4.9%, more than
155bp above its German equivalent, compared with a spread of
around 70bp at the end of 2025. Admittedly, the increase in market

yields feeds only gradually into the eﬀective cost of the debt as

through. Fitch estimates that France would need to reduce its
primary deﬁcit from around 2.9% of GDP this year to just 0.4% to
stabilise the debt ratio. The adjustment required would become
considerably larger if the eﬀective interest rate on the debt

gradually converged towards current market yields.
And the 2027 presidential election is unlikely to provide an easy
reset. A new president could again ﬁnd her or himself without a
parliamentary majority, making the large and sustained ﬁscal
adjustment required increasingly diﬃcult to deliver. These concerns
prompted Fitch to take the unusual step on 30 October of bringing
forward its assessment and revising the outlook on France’s A+
rating from stable to negative, only a week after Moody’s

unexpectedly kept its Aa3 rating unchanged, albeit with a negative
outlook. Fitch explicitly pointed to weaker growth, higher borrowing
costs and a wider deﬁcit, together with political fragmentation and
social tensions that make sustained ﬁscal consolidation harder to
achieve. It expects French public debt to reach around 128% of GDP
by 2030, considerably above the roughly 123% projected by the
IMF.
All in all, tomorrow’s demonstrations will once again underline the

population’s frustration over purchasing power. But without
stronger economic growth to lift household incomes while
simultaneously easing the pressure on the public ﬁnances, the
government has very limited room to oﬀer measures large enough
to make a meaningful diﬀerence without making an already
worrying ﬁscal trajectory even worse.

Document 362: GLOBAL MARKETS CALL_ Air Coming Out Of AI Bubble

Source PDF: all report pdf/GLOBAL MARKETS CALL_ Air Coming Out Of AI Bubble.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-08
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: GLOBAL MARKETS CALL: Air Coming Out Of AI Bubble
Date:    1 hour ago
GLOBAL MARKETS CALL: Air Coming Out Of AI Bubble

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
GLOBAL MARKETS CALL: Air
Coming Out Of AI Bubble
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 8 Nov 2026
View in browser
Photo by Gaël Gaborel - OrbisTerrae / Unsplash

### Page 2

The AI selloff went global last week. In the US, Democrats swept
Congress in the midterm elections on Tuesday, November 3, surfing on a
huge wave of voter hostility toward new data centers. On Thursday,
investors dumped the AI trade worldwide, and Asia’s chipmakers took the
brunt of the wipeout. Overseas earnings are still climbing, but valuations
keep falling. We see the AI backlash as a speed bump, not a roadblock, at
home and abroad. Here’s more:

I. Stock Market Performance
Global sector leadership flipped this week according to the FTSE indexes
(chart). Defensive sectors held up best, with Real Estate up 2.2% and
Consumer Staples up 0.7%, while Consumer Discretionary led with a
2.7% gain. Technology (-6.7%) and Telecommunications (-7.4%) were
near the bottom. Energy was the worst performer, down 10.4%, as fears
of a post-midterm re-escalation in the Middle East faded. The FTSE World
index fell 2.5%.

The US has been gaining ground since we lowered our "Go Global"
investment strategy to market weight on October 18. The US MSCI’s
share of the All Country World MSCI’s market cap has climbed to 65.3%,
up from 62.4% in March (chart). The gains have come at the expense of
everyone else. The Emerging Markets MSCI’s share has dropped to
11.1% from 12.1% in mid-October.

### Page 3

The AI-heavy markets were at the bottom of the country ETF rankings this
week (chart). Taiwan fell 11.7% in dollars, South Korea 10.4%, and Japan
5.0%. This year’s laggards caught bids. South Africa led with an 8.5%
gain, followed by India at 6.6%. The US ETF fell 2.7%, slightly worse than
the All Country World ex-US ETF’s 1.8% decline.
Both the US and overseas ETFs have pulled back from their recent highs
(chart). Both remain in solid uptrends that began in late 2022.

### Page 4

II. Stock Market Earnings & Valuation
The All Country World ex-US MSCI’s forward earnings rose to yet another
record high this week (chart). It has soared since 2025 after going
nowhere for most of the previous decade.
Forward earnings is up 41.0% y/y, while forward revenues is up 11.2%
(chart). During solid economic expansions, forward revenue growth tends
to hover around 5%. Today’s double-digit pace confirms an economic
boom underpinning the global bull market.

### Page 5

Valuations continue to fall even as earnings soar (chart). The US MSCI’s
forward P/E is down to 18.9. Japan’s has dropped to 14.2 from 14.7 a
week ago. The All Country World ex-US trades at 12.0. The Emerging
Markets MSCI has slipped to single digits and is at 8.6 now.
III. Bonds
Sovereign bonds continued to rally worldwide this week as investors fled
the AI trade (chart). The US 10-year Treasury yield fell to 4.86%. The UK's
yield remains the highest in the group, at 5.16%, followed by Australia’s

5.00%. France’s yield has dropped to 4.77%, back below the US yield.
Japan’s yield is down to 2.63%.

### Page 6

IV. Focus on South Korea & Taiwan
South Korea's forward earnings is still rising to record highs, though more
slowly than earlier in the year (chart). The stock market isn’t buying it. The
MSCI South Korea stock price index is down more than 45% from its
record high. Its forward P/E has collapsed to 3.4, the lowest in over 20
years. Investors are betting that analysts will have to slash their earnings
estimates.
Taiwan tells the same story, but with a more sensible valuation. Its forward

earnings is also at a record high. Its forward P/E has dropped to 14.4 from
23.2 earlier this year (chart).

### Page 7

Fundamental data suggest South Korea and Taiwan should continue to
benefit from US demand for technology hardware. US imports of selected
technologies rose to a record $61.3 billion in September (chart).
Asia supplies much of that hardware. US imports from Vietnam rose to
$28.7 billion in September, and imports from Taiwan rose to $28.4 billion
(chart). Imports from South Korea rose to $19.2 billion. A slower US data
center buildout would delay that demand, not derail it. We would view a

slower pace as more sustainable over a longer period of time.

clause.

Document 384: MiniMax创始人罕见公开访谈：将一直开源直到实现AGI，盈利靠压低推理成本不靠涨价

Source PDF: all report pdf/MiniMax创始人罕见公开访谈：将一直开源直到实现AGI，盈利靠压低推理成本不靠涨价.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 29
Status: ok

### Page 1

MiniMax 创始⼈闫俊杰⾸次英⽂播客亮相，抛出惊⼈论断： AI 盈利靠降成本⽽⾮涨价，低
推理成本既是收⼊护城河，也能放⼤模型后训练规模。截⾄ 8 ⽉，公司 ARR 已超 8 亿美元，
B2B 占⽐逾 80% 。他还宣布将坚持开源直⾄ AGI 实现 —— 因为 " 智能应属于所有⼈ " 。
摘要：
中国 AI 公司正在尝试⾛出⼀条与硅⾕不同的道路。 MiniMax 创始⼈、董事⻓兼 CEO 闫俊杰（ IO ）
在⾸次英⽂播客访谈中表⽰，公司将坚持开源，直到实现通⽤⼈⼯智能（ AGI ）；⽽实现盈利的
关键，不是提⾼模型价格，⽽是通过技术创新持续压低推理成本。
在他看来，美国 AI ⽣态依靠巨额资本和算⼒资源推动模型能⼒突破，中国公司则必须在有限的算
⼒条件下提⾼效率。 MiniMax 希望证明，即使⽆法获得同等规模的顶级芯⽚，也可以通过模型架
构、基础设施和算法优化，缩⼩与美国前沿模型的差距。
这套思路已经开始反映在商业模式上。闫俊杰透露，截⾄ 8 ⽉， MiniMax 的年度经常性收⼊年化

运⾏率（ ARR ）已超过 8 亿美元，其中企业业务占⽐超过 80% 。他认为，低推理成本不仅可以让
客⼾以更低价格使⽤ AI ，还能让公司以更低成本进⾏强化学习和后训练，从⽽形成商业增⻓与
模型能⼒提升相互促进的循环。
与此同时， MiniMax 正在推进语⾔模型与视频⽣成模型的融合，希望让 AI 从⽣成⽂本和视频⽚
段，进⼀步发展为能够规划、执⾏并完成复杂项⽬的智能体。闫俊杰强调， AGI 不应该属于某⼀
家企业、某⼀个国家或某个组织，⽽应该属于所有⼈。
华尔街⻅闻整理要点如下：
1 、 MiniMax 将坚持开源，直到 AGI 实现。闫俊杰认为，开源不是商业变现的替代⽅案，⽽是推
动商业化和技术创新的催化剂。开发者可以基于模型开发新产品，形成反馈循环，帮助模型更快
迭代。
2 、低推理成本是 MiniMax 商业模式的核⼼护城河。闫俊杰认为， AI 盈利不应该依赖涨价，⽽应
该通过技术创新降低推理成本。成本下降既能吸引更多客⼾，也能让公司以更低成本扩⼤后训练
规模。
3 、中国和美国的 AI ⽣态正在形成不同的发展路径。美国依靠资本、芯⽚和算⼒资源推动前沿模

型发展，中国公司则更重视单位算⼒的使⽤效率。资源约束反⽽促使中国 AI 实验室在架构、基础
设施和算法上持续创新。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

从资源约束到效率优势： MiniMax 选择了⼀条不同的 AI 道路
对于 MiniMax ⽽⾔，资源约束并⾮⼀个可以轻易消除的短期问题，⽽是从创⽴之初就必须⾯对的
现实。
美国头部 AI 公司拥有更充⾜的资本、芯⽚和算⼒资源，可以通过⼤量实验寻找最优技术路线。中
国公司则必须更加谨慎地使⽤每⼀单位算⼒。这种差异逐渐塑造了 MiniMax 的组织⽂化和技术战
略。
闫俊杰将公司的⽬标概括为：最⼤限度地降低推理成本，同时让每个⼈都能够享受到前沿智
能。
他认为， AI ⾏业最终的竞争不只是模型性能的竞争，也是单位算⼒能够创造多少智能的竞争。
开源不是放弃商业化，⽽是建⽴更⼤的创新⽹络
在中美 AI 公司的发展路径中，开源与闭源已经成为⼀个重要分⽔岭。
OpenAI 、 Anthropic 等美国公司主要采取闭源模式，通过控制模型访问权限和 API 服务实现商业
化。中国 AI 公司则更加积极地采⽤开放权重模式，允许开发者下载模型，并在⾃⼰的硬件上部署
和修改。
MiniMax 选择站在后者⼀边。闫俊杰表⽰，开源并不是商业变现的替代⽅案，反⽽是推动商业

化和进⼀步创新的催化剂。
他以公司 H3 模型为例。按照他的介绍， H3 发布后的前两周，已经有⼤约 300 个基于该模型构建
的开放模型出现。
其中，⼀家美国公司 fal.ai 基于 H3 进⾏后训练，开发出 H3 Max ，能够在 3 秒内⽣成 5 秒的视频，
实现接近实时的视频⽣成。
这意味着，开源模型的价值并不局限于原始模型本⾝。外部开发者可以在其基础上进⾏优化，
开发出原本没有被设想过的应⽤。
闫俊杰举例称，开发者已经利⽤相关技术搭建了 24 ⼩时运⾏的 AI 直播系统，内容可以根据⽤⼾
需求实时⽣成。类似的能⼒还可能延伸⾄实时游戏、教育和其他交互式体验。
4 、 MiniMax 正在押注语⾔模型与视频模型的融合。公司⽬前拥有 M 系列语⾔模型和 H 系列多模态
模型，未来希望将两者整合为统⼀系统，让 AI 从⽣成内容升级为完成⼴告、影视、设计等复杂⼯
作。
5 、 AI Agent 的下⼀阶段不只是执⾏任务，⽽是⾃主设定⽬标并验证结果。闫俊杰认为，真正有
价值的智能体应当具备⻓期任务中的⾃主性。

6 、 MiniMax 的竞争优势不仅在于技术，也在于创始⼈的资本配置能⼒。 Scottish Mortgage 投资
经理 Lawrence Burns 认为，闫俊杰能够减少⽆效实验、作出关键技术选择，并以更少的资源取
得较好的研发成果。
7 、盈利仍⾯临价格竞争和前沿研发成本上升两⼤挑战。 Lawrence Burns 指出，中国 AI 市场竞争
激烈， MiniMax 仍需持续投⼊以保持技术前沿，市场空间虽然巨⼤，但盈利能⼒和利润率仍需验
证。

### Page 3

对于企业和政府客⼾，开源还有另⼀个优势：模型可以部署在⾃⼰的硬件上，从⽽更好地保护
数据和隐私。因此， MiniMax 希望通过开放模型权重，让开发者、⽤⼾和模型公司形成⼀个相互
促进的⽣态。
闫俊杰甚⾄明确表⽰，公司计划⼀直开源，直到 AGI 实现。
这并不意味着开源模式没有商业化问题。模型下载量和开发者数量最终仍需要转化为 API 收⼊、
企业客⼾和实际使⽤量。但 MiniMax 的判断是，开放⽣态能够扩⼤模型的应⽤范围，并让公司以
更低成本获得更多技术反馈。
盈利不靠涨价：低推理成本同时决定收⼊与研发效率
相⽐开源战略，闫俊杰对 MiniMax 商业模式的解释更加值得关注。
⽬前， AI ⾏业普遍⾯临较⾼的研发和算⼒投⼊，很多公司仍处于亏损状态。对于 MiniMax ⽽⾔，
如何在保持模型能⼒增⻓的同时实现盈利，是投资者最关⼼的问题之⼀。
闫俊杰的答案是：AI 的利润不是通过提⾼价格获得的，⽽是通过创新降低推理成本获得的。
他认为，低推理成本同时构成公司的收⼊护城河和利润护城河。
⼀⽅⾯，推理成本下降意味着 MiniMax 可以⽤更低价格向客⼾提供服务，扩⼤市场覆盖范围。并

不是所有企业都需要全球最先进的模型，很多⽇常办公、编程和业务流程，只需要性能⾜够
好、价格合理的模型。
如果模型性能达到业务需求，⽽价格明显低于竞争对⼿，企业采⽤ AI 的投资回报率就会提⾼。
另⼀⽅⾯，低推理成本还会影响模型本⾝的研发效率。
闫俊杰特别强调了后训练的重要性。在模型完成初始训练之后，还需要通过监督微调、强化学
习和数据合成等⽅式，让模型不断练习、纠正错误并提⾼复杂任务处理能⼒。
这些过程都需要⼤量推理计算。因此，推理成本越低，公司就越有能⼒扩⼤后训练规模，让模
型获得更多练习机会。
Lawrence Burns 对此给出了⼀个⾼尔夫球的⽐喻：两名球⼿都需要不断练习才能进步，但如果
其中⼀⼈购买练习球的成本更低，那么在相同预算下，他就能进⾏更多练习，最终更有可能成
为更好的球⼿。
MiniMax 的优势就在于拥有更便宜的 “ 练习球 ” 。这意味着，低成本并不只是⼀个定价优势，还可
能转化为模型能⼒提升的优势。
闫俊杰透露，截⾄ 8 ⽉， MiniMax 的 ARR 已经超过 8 亿美元，其中 B2B 业务占⽐超过 80% 。

不过， ARR 是按照当前收⼊⽔平年化计算的运⾏率，并不等于已经实现的全年收⼊。
对于 MiniMax ⽽⾔，理想的发展路径是：推理成本持续下降，吸引更多客⼾使⽤，收⼊规模扩
⼤，再将资源投⼊后训练和模型研发，最终推动收⼊增⻓快于成本增⻓。这也是公司希望建⽴
的增⻓⻜轮。

### Page 4

中国 AI 公司的另⼀种竞争⽅式：单位算⼒创造更多智能
在谈及中美 AI 产业竞争时，闫俊杰认为，两种⽣态系统正在形成不同的发展模式。
美国 AI 公司拥有更多资本、算⼒和芯⽚资源，能够通过⼤规模计算推动模型能⼒突破。中国公司
则需要更加重视每单位算⼒的效率。
他表⽰，美国的 AI ⽣态正在建⽴由巨额资本⽀撑的封闭系统，⽽中国的 AI ⽣态则正在建⽴由效率
驱动的协作体系。
他并不认为两种⽣态最终会⾛向完全相同的道路。这种差异不仅体现在商业模式上，也体现在
技术架构和研发⽅法上。
MiniMax 重点投⼊三个领域：模型架构、基础设施和优化技术。闫俊杰表⽰，公司从⼀开始就
希望模型能够在不同芯⽚上保持良好的运⾏表现，⽽不是完全依赖某⼀种硬件。
在他看来，算⼒越受限制，算法和推理效率的重要性就越⾼。
这种资源约束反⽽让公司的⼯程师和研究⼈员变得更加优秀。这也解释了为什么 MiniMax 将企业
⽂化概括为扁平、专注和节俭。
公司⽬前只有约 500 名员⼯，因此必须把资源集中在最重要的事情上。

闫俊杰提出了两项研发原则：第⼀，让事情尽可能简单；第⼆，始终建设基础设施。他认为，
直接推出产品可能更快，但⻓期来看，基础设施才是实现规模化发展的基础。
Scottish Mortgage 投资经理 Lawrence Burns 则认为，闫俊杰的⼀个突出优势，是他能够同时扮
演优秀科学家和资本配置者的⻆⾊。
在很多美国 AI 实验室中，研究⼈员可以利⽤充⾜的资本和芯⽚资源开展⼤量实验。但 MiniMax 没
有这样的条件，因此必须减少⽆效尝试，尽早判断哪些技术路线值得投⼊。
Burns 认为，闫俊杰能够更准确地判断技术⽅向，并在较少的实验选择中取得较好的结果。这不
仅提⾼了资本效率，也有助于吸引和留住中国顶尖 AI ⼈才。
从视频⽣成到完整任务：语⾔模型与视频模型的融合
MiniMax 并不打算只做语⾔模型或者视频⽣成模型。公司⽬前拥有两个主要模型系列： M 系列语
⾔模型，以及 H 系列多模态模型。
M 系列主要⾯向编程、智能体和⽣产⼒应⽤； H 系列则专注于视频⽣成，并能够处理图像和⾳

频。
闫俊杰表⽰，公司希望在未来将两个系列整合为⼀个统⼀模型。
这⼀战略背后，是他对 AI 应⽤形态变化的判断。
⽬前的视频⽣成模型能够根据提⽰词⽣成视频⽚段，但在理解复杂指令、根据反馈修改内容以
及完成连续任务⽅⾯仍有不⾜。

### Page 5

例如，在⼴告、电影和设计⼯作中，⽤⼾不仅需要⽣成⼀段视频，还需要不断调整镜头、修改
内容、理解创作意图，并最终完成⼀个完整项⽬。
这就需要语⾔模型的理解、规划和执⾏能⼒与视频模型的⽣成能⼒结合起来。 Lawrence Burns
认为，这种融合可能打开⽐单纯⽣成视频⽚段更⼤的市场。
当 AI 能够从⽣成内容升级为完成整个项⽬时，企业愿意⽀付的费⽤也可能显著提⾼。
与此同时， MiniMax 还在押注 AI Agent 。闫俊杰认为，下⼀代智能体的竞争重点，不只是能否完
成复杂任务，⽽是能否⾃主制定⽬标，并验证任务结果。
⽬前， MiniMax 已经将编程和 Agent 能⼒作为 M 系列模型的核⼼⽅向。他还特别提到，⾃⼰当时
正集中精⼒研发下⼀代模型 M3 Pro ，并认为它有望成为⼀项重要突破。
盈利仍⾯临两⼤挑战：价格战与前沿研发成本
尽管 MiniMax 已经展现出较快的收⼊增⻓，但盈利仍然是公司必须⾯对的问题。
Lawrence Burns 指出，今年上半年， MiniMax 收⼊接近增⻓四倍，但报告亏损规模也达到原来

的约三倍。
他认为，公司实现盈利⾯临两项主要挑战。
第⼀，中国 AI 市场的价格竞争⾮常激烈。
MiniMax 需要与 DeepSeek 等竞争对⼿争夺客⼾，⽽激烈的价格竞争可能压低整个⾏业的服务价
格。
第⼆，维持前沿模型能⼒的成本仍在不断上升。
随着 AI 模型不断进步，训练和推理所需的资源也在增加。这也是 MiniMax 在 7 ⽉份融资 20 亿美元
的原因之⼀。
在 Burns 看来，公司需要依靠精简的成本结构、正确的技术选择，以及能够⼤规模变现的市场来
应对这些挑战。
但他也指出，⽬前已经出现了⼀些积极变化：研发⽀出的增⻓速度开始低于销售收⼊增⻓速
度，企业业务收⼊也在快速扩⼤。
按照他的介绍，企业业务收⼊增⻓约⼋倍，⽬前占总收⼊的 63% 。⽽闫俊杰提到的超过 80% ，
则是按年化⼝径计算的业务占⽐。
企业客⼾的增加有助于改善商业化能⼒，因为随着模型性能提升，企业可以利⽤ AI 完成更多⼯
作，也更愿意为更⾼的⽣产⼒⽀付费⽤。
不过， Burns 强调， MiniMax 仍然是⼀项早期阶段的投资，最终结果存在较⼤的不确定性。这也

是基⾦控制其投资组合持仓规模的原因之⼀。
AGI 不属于任何⼀家企业： MiniMax 的最终愿景

### Page 6

访谈最后， Lawrence Burns 询问闫俊杰，如果 MiniMax 实现⾃⼰的⽬标，世界将会发⽣怎样的
改变。
闫俊杰给出的回答是：真正的问题不是 AGI 能否实现，⽽是 AGI 最终将让谁受益。
在他看来， AGI 不应该属于某⼀家企业、某⼀个国家或者某个组织，⽽应该属于所有⼈。
他认为，使⽤模型的⼈、基于模型开发应⽤的开发者，以及为模型作出贡献的研究⼈员，都应
该成为 AGI ⽣态的⼀部分。
这也是 MiniMax 将公司⼝号定为 “Intelligence with everyone” （与所有⼈共享智能），⽽不
是 “Intelligence for everyone” （为所有⼈提供智能）的原因。
两者看似只有⼀个词的区别，背后的含义却不同。
前者强调智能不仅应该服务于所有⼈，更应该由所有⼈共同参与和拥有。
对于 MiniMax ⽽⾔，开源不仅是⼀种技术策略，也是⼀种关于 AI 未来归属的选择。
⽽对于投资者来说，最终需要验证的仍然是另⼀件事：这种开放和低成本的路线，能否在激烈
的⾏业竞争中形成可持续的商业回报。

以下为访谈原⽂，由 AI 辅助翻译：
这场访谈由英国投资信托 Scottish Mortgage 旗下播客《 Invest in Progress 》推出，也是
MiniMax 创始⼈闫俊杰⾸次接受英⽂播客采访。
对话分为两个部分。第⼀部分由 Scottish Mortgage 投资经理 Lawrence Burns 与闫俊杰交流，围
绕 MiniMax 的技术路线、商业模式、开源战略以及 AGI 愿景展开；第⼆部分则由节⽬主持⼈
Claire Shaw 采访 Lawrence Burns ，进⼀步解释基⾦投资 MiniMax 的原因、公司的竞争优势以及
潜在⻛险。
对话嘉宾：
闫俊杰（ Yan Junjie ， IO ）： MiniMax 创始⼈、董事⻓兼 CEO ，机器学习和计算机视觉研究背
景，曾在百度深度学习研究院⼯作，是公司技术战略的主要推动者。
Lawrence Burns： Scottish Mortgage 投资经理，参与 MiniMax 投资研究和决策，重点关注
公司的技术路线、资本效率与⻓期商业化潜⼒。
Claire Shaw： Scottish Mortgage 投资组合总监，也是《 Invest in Progress 》主持⼈，负责
串联访谈，并在最后与 Lawrence Burns 讨论投资逻辑。
开场： MiniMax 为什么⾛出了⼀条不同的  AI 道路
Claire Shaw （主持⼈，节⽬旁⽩）：
对于我们⽽⾔，尤其是对于⼀家中国  AI 公司来说，算⼒⼀直是⼀种约束。
但这种约束也让我们成为了更优秀的⼯程师和研究⼈员。

### Page 7

Lawrence Burns （投资经理，后期回顾）：
去年 12 ⽉，我第⼀次⻅到  IO 。当时，我们对他所介绍的情况，以及中国  AI ⽣态的发展都⾮常感
兴趣。
按照他的说法， MiniMax 已经能够以领先前沿模型⼗分之⼀的成本，实现约 95% 的性能。
我认为，美国的  AI ⽣态正在建⽴由巨额资本⽀撑的封闭系统，⽽中国的  AI ⽣态则正在建⽴⼀
种由效率驱动的协作体系。
这种低成本模式与我们在美国看到的模式有所不同，⽽且它在全球范围内可能具有很⾼的价值。
⾸先，并不是所有⼯作都需要使⽤最顶尖的模型。
其次，并不是所有⽤⼾和企业都像美国和欧洲的⽤⼾、企业那样富有，能够负担⾼昂的费⽤。
闫俊杰（ IO ）：
AGI 不应该属于某⼀家企业、某⼀个国家或者某个组织。
它必须属于我们所有⼈。
节⽬介绍：⼀家不⼀样的中国  AI 公司
Claire Shaw ：
⼤家好，欢迎收听  Scottish Mortgage 播客节⽬《 Invest in Progress 》。我是  Claire Shaw ，担

任投资组合总监。
在这档节⽬中，我们将带⼤家⾛进幕后，聆听我们的投资经理与全球最优秀的成⻓型企业领导者
之间的对话。
作为⼀家英国投资信托基⾦， Scottish Mortgage 只能在特定的受众群体和地理区域内进⾏推
⼴。因此，请⼤家查看播客介绍，确认本期节⽬是否适合您收听。与任何投资⼀样，您的本⾦都
存在⻛险。
MiniMax 是⼀家业务覆盖全球、专注于⼈⼯智能的公司。它与我们持有的另⼀家投资组合公司
Anthropic 有⼀些共同之处。
但这家中国企业与美国同⾏有着截然不同的特点。
MiniMax 的⾸席执⾏官是闫俊杰博⼠，也被称为  IO 。
正如接下来⼤家将听到的，他奉⾏的理念是：以尽可能低的成本提供智能。
⽬前， MiniMax 既⽆法获得⼤量最先进的计算芯⽚，也没有⾜够的预算来购买这些芯⽚。因
此，它不得不学会⽤更少的资源做更多的事情。
尽管⾯临这些限制， IO 很早就决定， MiniMax 的模型不能只⽣成⽂本，还应该具备视频和⾳频
⽣成能⼒。
所以，如果你曾经在  YouTube 上看过  AI ⽣成的视频，那么很可能  MiniMax 就参与了其中。

### Page 8

此外， MiniMax 还是开源模式的积极倡导者。
Anthropic 选择将模型保持封闭，并向客⼾收取使⽤费⽤；⽽  MiniMax 则允许⽤⼾下载其技术，
并在⾃⼰的硬件上运⾏。
这意味着，⽤⼾实际上可以免费使⽤  MiniMax 的模型，甚⾄可以根据⾃⼰的需求进⾏修改。
乍⼀看，这似乎不是⼀种特别好的商业模式。但实际上，这种⽅式能够扩⼤⽤⼾群体，同时帮助
公司以更低的成本、更快的速度进⾏创新和改进产品。
归根结底，⼈⼯智能竞赛并不只发⽣在硅⾕。
MiniMax 为  Scottish Mortgage 提供了⼀个投资机会，让我们能够参与⼀家采⽤不同发展路径的
竞争者。
这是  IO ⾸次接受英⽂播客采访。提前提醒⼀下，他会使⽤⼀些专业术语。如果你对推理
（ Inference ）、通⽤⼈⼯智能（ AGI ）或者强化学习（ Reinforcement Learning ）不太熟悉，也
不⽤担⼼，节⽬说明中准备了专业术语解释。
接下来，让我们听听投资经理  Lawrence Burns 与  IO 的对话。

⼀个⽉内，它的下载量可能达到了 2500 万次。

MiniMax 如何实现盈利？低推理成本才是真正的护城河

### Page 15

闫俊杰（ IO ）：

⾸先，我想说，最后那 5% 的差距，实际上可能⽐最初达到 95% 要困难得多。

但因为我们仍然处于  AGI 发展的早期阶段，所以真正重要的是我们能够以多快的速度进步。
对于我们⽽⾔，尤其是对于⼀家中国  AI 公司来说，算⼒始终是⼀种约束。
因此，从第⼀天开始，我们就知道必须尽可能提⾼推理效率。

我们关注的是进步速度，⽽不是某⼀个时间点的排名。
这也促使我们在模型架构和基础设施⽅⾯不断创新，⽽不仅仅依赖芯⽚本⾝。

### Page 16
